Projected gradient for max:
- At each outer iteration, compute c = fft_conv(a, a), max M, active indices where c > M - tol. - Direction: want decrease M and maintain sum=1. Subgradient g (nonnegative). The linear decrease if move from high g to low g: d_i = -g_i (then project to tangent sum zero and nonnegative). Alternatively, do pairwise swaps: find i to donate (high a_i and high a_{k*-i}) and j to receive (low a_{k*-j}) with a_i>0. Transfer δ min(a_i, target). This is like coordinate. Need O(n) to find. Could be simple and memory linear.

Maybe use "deflation": Since peak at lag k*, the peak is sum_i a_i a_{k*-i}. To reduce it, we can lower a_i where a_{k*-i} high. But lowering must preserve sum by increasing elsewhere where product small. We can compute P_i = a_i * a_{k*-i}. The peak = sum P_i. Gradient says marginal reduction per unit moved from i to j is 2(a_{j?}?). If move δ from i to j, change c_k ≈ 2δ(a_{k*-j} - a_{k*-i}) plus second order. So choose i with large a_{k*-i} (and a_i>0) and j with small a_{k*-j}. Also need avoid increasing other lags maybe.

This suggests algorithm:
- Normalize a sum=1.
- Iterate:
   - Compute conv and max k*.
   - Compute y_i = a_{k*-i} (valid reflected array length n). For invalid set 0.
   - We want move mass from donors i with high y_i and a_i high to receivers j with low y_j.
   - Could sort by y? O(n log n), fine 4806. But maybe simple: use quantiles.
   - Compute score improvement by line search with a modified vector:
       new = a.copy()
       Select donors top D by a_i*y_i maybe, receivers bottom R by y_j.
       Total mass m to move. Transfer proportionally? e.g., decrease donors by α*δ_i, increase receivers by α*m weighted 1/(1+new?) maintaining nonneg.
       Try α values [0.1,0.25,0.5,...] and evaluate cost (FFT) exact.
   - Accept best if improves max or score.
   - Also do random perturbations and symmetrize? Could get stuck.

Algorithm multiplicative gradient for M (smooth max):
- a >=0, sum=1.
- Compute C via FFT.
- Compute weights w on lags: e.g., softmax(C/τ) or active set.
- Compute h_i = ∑_k w_k C? no, g_i = ∑_k w_k a_{k-i} (via conv(w, rev(a))).
- Update log a_i ← log a_i - η * (g_i - mean? to preserve sum approximately). If we subtract η*g, sum changes. Then normalize. Multiplicative: a_i ← a_i / (1+η*g_i) maybe. Since derivative of c_k w.r.t log a_i = a_{k-i}. We want minimize max; reduce a_i with high g_i. Then renormalize to sum=1. This is like projected gradient on log simplex. Let's design practical local search:
- Represent a normalized to sum=1. Use exact score = 2n*max(C) (since sum=1). Evaluator will scale? If we return scaled to maybe [0,1000] with sum large, score invariant. During optimization sum=1.
- Use FFT convolution function fft_conv(a): returns full conv length 2n-1. Need efficient. Using numpy: m = len(fft) as power of two >=2n-1. Use rfft. c = irfft(rfft(a, m)*rfft(a,m)). c[:2n-1]. Clean tiny negatives to 0. Since a nonneg, conv nonneg but numerical small negative possible.

- Multiplicative candidate:
   ```

Pseudo:
```
import numpy as np, time

def next_pow2(m):
    L=1
    while L<m: L <<=1
    return L

def normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
    return a/s

def convolve_max(a, ffac):
    n=a.size
    af=np.fft.rfft(a,n=ffac)
    c=np.fft.irfft(af*af,n=ffac)
    len_c=2*n-1
    c=c[:len_c]
    # remove tiny negative
    if c[0] <0 or c[-1]<0: c=np.maximum(c,0)
    return c

def convolve_weighted(w, a, ffac2, n):
    r=a[::-1]
    wf=np.fft.rfft(w,n=ffac2)
    rf=np.fft.rfft(r,n=ffac2)
    h=np.fft.irfft(wf*rf,n=ffac2)
    shift=n-1
    return h[shift:shift+n]

def softmax_weights(c, tau):
    cmax=c.max()
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax<=0: return np.ones_like(c)/c.size
    z=(c-cmax)/tau
    exp=np.exp(z)
    s=exp.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[c>=cmax-1e-12*max(1,cmax)]=1/(...)
    else: w=exp/s
    return w
```

```
def softmax_lag_weights(c, tau):
    cmax=float(c.max())
    m=c.size
    if cmax <= 0:
        w=np.zeros(m); w[0]=1; return w
    if tau <= 0:
        w=np.zeros(m); w[c >= cmax - 0.5] =1; return w/w.sum()
    z=(c-cmax)/tau
    z=np.clip(z, -745, 0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       w=np.zeros(m); idx=(c>=cmax-1e-12*max(1.0,cmax)); w[idx]=1; s=w.sum(); if s<=0 w[0]=1 else w/=s
    else: w=e/s
    return w
```
Clipping z to -745 avoids underflow to 0 but exp(-745) ~5e-324, okay. Actually np.exp(-745) may underflow to 5e-324; fine. Could clip -50 to avoid many tiny? Use -50; if weights too few? tau=0.1M, z min about -50 if 10% below? We want include lags near max; -50 e^-50 negligible. Good. Use -50.

def initial_candidates(n, parent):
    out=[]
    idx=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base=1.0+idx
    out.append(np.ones(n))
    out.append(base)
    for p in (-0.55,-0.5,-0.45,-0.3,0.0,0.3,1.0):
       arr=np.power(base, p)
       out.append(arr)
    if parent is not None and np.any(parent):
       out.append(parent)
    # maybe triangular? arr=np.minimum(idx+1, n-idx)?? symmetric bad but local.
    # random? no deterministic.
    return out
```
For p negative, power base positive. For p=1 ramp. For p=0 ones duplicate. Fine.

Normalize function:
```
def normalize_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size ==0: return a
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       a=np.ones_like(a); s=a.size
    return a/s
```
Using np.where creates array. Good.

Now in propose:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        parent = None
    if parent is None or parent.size ==0:
        n=256
    else:
        parent = np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
        parent=np.clip(parent,0,None)
        n=int(parent.size)
    # if n too small/large? If n==0 n=1
    if n < 1: n=1
    # If n extremely large, maybe limit? But memory. Assume okay.
    init_list=initial_candidates(n, parent if parent is not None else None)
    ffac=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    ffac2=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    best=None; best_sc=np.inf
    for arr in init_list:
       a=normalize_array(arr)
       if a.size==n:
          c=full_convolution_fft(a, ffac); M=float(c.max()); sc=2.0*n*M
          if finite and sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best=a.copy()
    if best is None: best=np.ones(n); best_sc=2.0
    a=best.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0.0, float(budget_s)-15.0)
    tau_frac=0.1
    stagnant=0
    best_tau_frac=tau_frac
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c=full_convolution_fft(a, ffac); M=float(c.max()); sc=2.0*n*M
       if not finite: break?
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_sc=sc; best=a.copy(); stagnant=0
       else:
           stagnant +=1
       if M <=0: break
       # adjust tau
       if stagnant > 80:
           tau_frac = 0.3 if tau_frac < 0.2 else 0.1
           if stagnant > 160:
              # random restart perturbation
              noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, size=n)
              b = normalize_array(a * np.exp(np.clip(noise, -2, 2)))
              cb=full_convolution_fft(b, ffac); Mbf=float(cb.max()); scb=2.0*n*Mbf
              if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < best_sc + 0.05:
                  a=b; cur_sc=scb
              stagnant=0
       tau = tau_frac * M
       w=softmax_lag_weights(c, tau)
       if w.size != c.size? no
       h=weighted_convolution(w, a, ffac2, n-1)  # need pass n?
       grad=(2.0/tau)*h
       # sanitize grad
       grad=np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
       grad=np.maximum(grad, 0.0)
       dot=float(np.dot(a, grad))
       rel=grad - dot
       rel=np.where(np.isfinite(rel), rel, 0.0)
       gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(rel))) if rel.size else 0.0
       improved=False
       if gmax > 1e-15:
          for maxlog in (0.05, 0.15, 0.4):
             eta=maxlog/gmax
             logit=-eta*rel
             logit=np.clip(logit, -10.0, 10.0)
             b=a*np.exp(logit)
             b=normalize_array(b)
             cb=full_convolution_fft(b, ffac); Mbf=float(cb.max()); scb=2.0*n*Mbf
             if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < sc - 1e-12:
                 a=b; sc=scb; improved=True
                 if sc < best_sc -1e-12: best_sc=sc; best=a.copy(); stagnant=0
                 break
       if not improved:
          # transfer
          D=min(32, n)
          contrib=a*grad
          # argpartition requires kth; if D==n, all
          if D < n:
             donors=np.argpartition(contrib, -D)[-D:]
             receivers=np.argpartition(grad, D)[:D]
          else:
             donors=np.arange(n); receivers=np.arange(n)
          # don't choose receivers with b currently high? fine
          for move_frac in (0.01, 0.03, 0.06):
             b=a.copy()
             don_mass=sum(b[donors])
             m=move_frac * don_mass  # fraction of donor mass? Or total mass? move_frac of total? b sum1, donor sum maybe fraction.
             # Use total mass fraction: min(move_frac, 0.5*don_mass)
             m=min(move_frac, 0.5*don_mass) if n>0 else 0
             if m <= 1e-15: continue
             wd=b[donors]; s=wd.sum()
             if s>0:
                b[donors] -= m*wd/s
             # receivers: equal, but avoid negative? b positive
             add=m/len(receivers)
             b[receivers] += add
             b=normalize_array(b)
             cb=full_convolution_fft(b, ffac); Mbf=float(cb.max()); scb=2.0*n*Mbf
             if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < sc -1e-12:
                a=b; sc=scb; improved=True
                if sc<best_sc-1e-12: best_sc=sc; best=a.copy(); stagnant=0
                break
       if not improved:
           # occasionally small additive random noise
           if (stagnant % 20 == 0):
              noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, size=n)
              b = normalize_array(a * np.exp(np.clip(noise, -1, 1)))
              cb=full_convolution_fft(b, ffac); Mbf=float(cb.max()); scb=2.0*n*Mbf
              if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < sc - 1e-12:
                 a=b; sc=scb
                 if sc<best_sc-1e-12: best_sc=sc; best=a.copy(); stagnant=0
       # Iteration guard? if sc worsen due current a? We only set a if improves sc. So current never worse.
       # If stagnant too high and random restart didn't help, maybe reset to best and switch tau
       if stagnant > 300:
           a=best.copy(); sc=best_sc; stagnant=0; tau_frac=0.05
```

Pseudo full code:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def fpow2(m):
    L=1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    return L

def normalize_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a.copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        return a / a.size
    return a / s

def full_conv(a, flen):
    n=a.size
    af=np.fft.rfft(a, n=flen)
    c=np.fft.irfft(af*af, n=flen)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c

def weighted_conv(w, a, flen, shift):
    r=a[::-1]
    wf=np.fft.rfft(w, n=flen)
    rf=np.fft.rfft(r, n=flen)
    out=np.fft.irfft(wf*rf, n=flen)
    n=a.size
    return out[shift:shift+n]

def lag_weights(c, tau):
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    m=c.size
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        w=np.zeros(m); w[0]=1.0; return w
    if tau <= 0.0:
        mask = c >= cmax - 1e-12*max(1.0,cmax)
        w=mask.astype(np.float64)
        s=w.sum()
        if s<=0: w[0]=1; s=1
        return w/s
    z=(c-cmax)/tau
    z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        mask = c >= cmax - 1e-8*max(1.0,cmax)
        w=mask.astype(np.float64); s=w.sum()
        if s<=0: w[0]=1; s=1
        return w/s
    return e/s

def sequence_score_normalized(a, flen):
    c=full_conv(a, flen)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M): return np.inf, c, M
    return 2.0*a.size*M, c, M

def initial_list(n, parent):
    out=[]
    idx=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base=1.0+idx
    out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    out.append(base)
    # exponents
    for p in (-0.6, -0.55, -0.5, -0.45, -0.4, -0.25, -0.1):
        out.append(np.power(base, p))
    for p in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0):
        out.append(np.power(base, p))
    if parent is not None:
        p=np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
        p=np.clip(p,0,None)
        if p.size==n and np.sum(p)>0:
            out.append(p)
    # perhaps triangular (symmetric) as extra
    # if n>2:
    #   x=idx/(n-1)
    #   out.append(np.abs(x-0.5)+1?) no
    return out
```
Exponents list 10 values okay. But for p negative large, base first 1, okay. For n=1, base [1], powers all 1, duplicates; okay.

Could include parent normalized. Good.

Helper:
```
def scale_for_return(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    m=float(np.max(a)) if a.size else 0
    if not finite or m<=0:
        a=np.ones_like(a); m=1
    b=a*(1000.0/m)
    b=np.clip(b,0,1000)
    s=b.sum()
    if s<0.01:
        return np.ones(a.size)*1.0
    return b
```

Let's write code now mentally:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def next_power_two(m):
    L=1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    return L

def normalize_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a.copy()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a=np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s=float(a.size)
    return a/s

def full_convolution(a, flen):
    n=a.size
    af=np.fft.rfft(a, n=flen)
    c=np.fft.irfft(af*af, n=flen)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c

def weighted_convolution(w, a, flen, shift):
    r=a[::-1]
    wf=np.fft.rfft(w, n=flen)
    rf=np.fft.rfft(r, n=flen)
    out=np.fft.irfft(wf*rf, n=flen)
    n=a.size
    return out[shift:shift+n]

def softmax_weights(c, tau):
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    m=c.size
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        w=np.zeros(m); w[0]=1.0; return w
    if tau <= 0.0:
        mask = c >= cmax - 1e-12*max(1.0, cmax)
        w = mask.astype(np.float64)
        s = float(np.sum(w))
        if s <= 0.0:
            w[0]=1.0; s=1.0
        return w/s
    z=(c-cmax)/tau
    z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        mask = c >= cmax - 1e-8*max(1.0, cmax)
        w=mask.astype(np.float64)
        s=float(np.sum(w))
        if s <= 0.0:
            w[0]=1.0; s=1.0
        return w/s
    return e/s

def score_of_sequence(a, flen):
    c=full_convolution(a, flen)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.inf, c, M
    return 2.0*float(a.size)*M, c, M

def initial_sequences(n, parent):
    res=[]
    idx=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base=1.0+idx
    res.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    res.append(base)
    for p in (-0.6, -0.55, -0.5, -0.45, -0.4, -0.3, -0.2):
        res.append(np.power(base, p))
    for p in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0):
        res.append(np.power(base, p))
    if parent is not None:
        p=np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
        p=np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
        if p.size == n and float(np.sum(p)) > 0.0:
            res.append(p)
    return res

def blend_score(x, y, t, flen):
    z=(1.0-t)*x + t*y
    z=normalize_sequence(z)
    sc, cc, mm=score_of_sequence(z, flen)
    return z, sc

def block_perturb(a, rng):
    n=a.size
    b=a.copy()
    if n > 1:
        lo=max(1, n//200)
        hi=max(2, n//20 + 1) # integers hi exclusive? rng.integers(lo, hi) requires hi>lo.
        if hi <= lo:
            hi = lo + 1
        width=int(rng.integers(lo, hi))
        start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        stop=min(n, start + width)
        factor=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12)))
        b[stop? no b[start:stop] *= factor]
    return normalize_sequence(b)

def scale_for_return(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    m=float(np.max(a)) if a.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        a=np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        m=1.0
    b=a*(1000.0/m)
    b=np.clip(b, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(np.sum(b)) < 0.01:
        b=np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return b
```
Fix block_perturb line.

def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    try:
       parent=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    if parent is not None:
       parent=np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
       if parent.ndim !=1:
          parent=parent.ravel()
       parent=np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
       parent=np.clip(parent,0,None)
    if parent is None or parent.size ==0:
       n=256
       parent=None
    else:
       n=int(parent.size)
    ffac=next_power_two(2*n-1)
    ffac2=next_power_two(3*n-2)
    best=None; best_sc=np.inf; inits=[]
    for arr in initial_sequences(n, parent):
       a=normalize_sequence(arr)
       if a.size != n: continue
       sc, c, M=score_of_sequence(a, ffac)
       if np.isfinite(sc):

    sc=best_sc
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start=time.monotonic()

       if stagnant > 80:

             if restarted: break

             scb,_,_=score_of_sequence(b, ffac)
             if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < best_sc + 0.05:
                a=b; sc=scb
          stagnant=0
       tau=tau_frac*M
       w=softmax_weights(c, tau)
       h=weighted_convolution(w, a, ffac2, n-1)
       grad=(2.0/tau)*h
       grad=np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
       grad=np.maximum(grad, 0.0)
       dot=float(np.dot(a, grad))
       rel=grad-dot
       rel=np.where(np.isfinite(rel), rel, 0.0)
       gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(rel))) if rel.size else 0.0
       improved=False
       if gmax > 1e-15:
          for maxlog in (0.05, 0.12, 0.3):
             eta=maxlog/gmax
             logit=-eta*rel
             logit=np.clip(logit, -10.0, 10.0)
             b=a*np.exp(logit)
             b=normalize_sequence(b)
             cb=full_convolution(b, ffac)
             Mb=float(np.max(cb))
             scb=2.0*float(n)*Mb
             if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < sc - 1e-12:
                a=b; sc=scb; improved=True
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                   best_sc=sc; best=a.copy(); stagnant=0
                break
       if not improved:
          D=min(32, n)
          if D > 0:
             contrib=a*grad
             if D < n:
                donors=np.argpartition(contrib, n-D)[-D:]
                receivers=np.argpartition(grad, D-1)[:D]
             else:
                donors=np.arange(n)
                receivers=np.arange(n)
             # Optionally filter donors with b>0; already.
             for move_frac in (0.008, 0.02, 0.05):
                b=a.copy()
                don_sum=float(np.sum(b[donors]))
                mmove=min(move_frac, 0.5*don_sum)
                if mmove <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                wd=b[donors]
                s=float(np.sum(wd))
                if s > 0.0:
                    b[donors] -= mmove*wd/s
                add=mmove/len(receivers)
                b[receivers] += add
                b=normalize_sequence(b)
                cb=full_convolution(b, ffac)
                Mb=float(np.max(cb))
                scb=2.0*float(n)*Mb
                if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < sc - 1e-12:

                   if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                      best_sc=sc; best=a.copy(); stagnant=0

       if not improved and stagnant % 40 == 0:
          b=block_perturb(a, rng)

    # final exact selection
    candidates=[]
    candidates.append(best)
    if parent is not None and parent.size == n and float(np.sum(parent)) > 0:
        candidates.append(parent)
    candidates.append(np.power(1.0+np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64), -0.5))
    candidates.append(np.arange(1.0,n+1,dtype=np.float64))
    final_best=None; final_sc=np.inf
    use_exact = n <= 20000
    for arr in candidates:
       if arr.size != n: continue
       scaled=scale_for_return(arr)
       if use_exact:
          try:
             sct=float(evaluate_sequence(scaled.tolist()))
          except Exception:
             sct=np.inf
       else:

          sct=sc_final

       final_best=scale_for_return(best)

       except Exception:
          sft=np.inf
       if not np.isfinite(sft):
          final_best=np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(x) for x in final_best]

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def next_power_two(m):
    L = 1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def normalize_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a.copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a / s


def full_convolution(a, flen):
    n = a.size
    af = np.fft.rfft(a, n=flen)
    c = np.fft.irfft(af * af, n=flen)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def weighted_convolution(w, a, flen, shift):
    r = a[::-1]
    wf = np.fft.rfft(w, n=flen)
    rf = np.fft.rfft(r, n=flen)
    out = np.fft.irfft(wf * rf, n=flen)
    n = a.size
    return out[shift:shift + n]


def softmax_weights(c, tau):
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    m = c.size
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[0] = 1.0
        return w
    if tau <= 0.0:
        mask = c >= cmax - 1e-12 * max(1.0, cmax)
        w = mask.astype(np.float64)
        s = float(np.sum(w))
        if s <= 0.0:
            w[0] = 1.0
            s = 1.0
        return w / s
    z = (c - cmax) / tau
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        mask = c >= cmax - 1e-8 * max(1.0, cmax)
        w = mask.astype(np.float64)
        s = float(np.sum(w))
        if s <= 0.0:
            w[0] = 1.0
            s = 1.0
        return w / s
    return e / s


def score_of_sequence(a, flen):
    c = full_convolution(a, flen)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.inf, c, M
    return 2.0 * float(a.size) * M, c, M


def initial_sequences(n, parent):
    res = []
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base = 1.0 + idx
    res.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    res.append(base.copy())

    for p in (-0.6, -0.55, -0.5, -0.45, -0.4, -0.3, -0.2):
        res.append(np.power(base, p))
    for p in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0):
        res.append(np.power(base, p))

    inv = np.power(base, -0.5)
    res.append(inv.copy())

    right_base = float(n) + 1.0 - idx
    for q in (0.1, 0.25, 0.4, 0.6):
        right = np.power(right_base, -q)
        for lam in (0.01, 0.05, 0.2):
            arr = inv + lam * right
            if float(np.sum(np.maximum(arr, 0.0))) > 0.0:
                res.append(np.maximum(arr, 0.0))

    if n > 1:
        x = idx / float(n - 1)
        for fac in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5):
            arr = inv * np.maximum(1.0 - fac * x, 0.05)
            if float(np.sum(arr)) > 0.0:
                res.append(arr)
            arr = inv * (1.0 + fac * x)
            if float(np.sum(arr)) > 0.0:
                res.append(arr)

    if parent is not None:
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
        if p.size == n and float(np.sum(p)) > 0.0:
            res.append(p)

    return res


def blend_score(x, y, t, flen):
    z = (1.0 - t) * x + t * y
    z = normalize_sequence(z)
    sc, _, _ = score_of_sequence(z, flen)
    return z, sc


def block_perturb(a, rng):
    n = a.size
    b = a.copy()
    if n > 1:
        lo = max(1, n // 200)
        hi = max(lo + 1, n // 20 + 1)
        width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        stop = min(n, start + width)
        factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12)))
        b[start:stop] *= factor
    return normalize_sequence(b)


def prepare_return(a):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(np.sum(a)) < 0.01:
        a = np.ones(max(1, int(a.size)), dtype=np.float64)
        a = a / float(a.size)
    return a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        if parent.ndim != 1:
            parent = parent.ravel()
        parent = np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
        parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, None)

    if parent is None or parent.size == 0:
        n = 256
        parent = None
    else:
        n = int(parent.size)

    if n < 1:
        n = 1
        parent = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    ffac = next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    ffac2 = next_power_two(3 * n - 2)

    best = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    inits = []

    for arr in initial_sequences(n, parent):
        a = normalize_sequence(arr)
        if a.size != n:
            continue
        sc, _, _ = score_of_sequence(a, ffac)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            inits.append(a.copy())
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = a.copy()

    if best is None:
        best = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        best_sc, _, _ = score_of_sequence(best, ffac)
        inits = [best.copy()]

    a = best.copy()
    sc = best_sc
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    start = time.monotonic()
    headroom = 30.0
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - headroom)

    target_score = 1.50274349
    target_M = target_score / (2.0 * float(n))

    tau_frac = 0.1
    stagnant = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = full_convolution(a, ffac)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            break
        sc = 2.0 * float(n) * M

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a.copy()
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1

        if M <= 0.0:
            break

        if stagnant > 80:
            tau_frac = 0.3 if tau_frac < 0.2 else 0.1

        if stagnant > 200:
            restarted = False
            for t in (0.3, 0.6):
                for init in inits:
                    b, scb = blend_score(best, init, t, ffac)
                    if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < best_sc + 0.03:
                        a = b
                        sc = scb
                        restarted = True
                        break
                if restarted:
                    break
            if not restarted:
                b = block_perturb(best, rng)
                scb, _, _ = score_of_sequence(b, ffac)
                if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < best_sc + 0.05:
                    a = b
                    sc = scb
            stagnant = 0

        if M > target_M:
            tau = max(0.05 * M, 0.25 * (M - target_M))
        else:
            tau = tau_frac * M
        tau = max(float(tau), 1e-12)

        w = softmax_weights(c, tau)
        h = weighted_convolution(w, a, ffac2, n - 1)
        grad = (2.0 / tau) * h
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
        grad = np.maximum(grad, 0.0)

        dot = float(np.dot(a, grad))
        rel = grad - dot
        rel = np.where(np.isfinite(rel), rel, 0.0)
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(rel))) if rel.size else 0.0

        cand = None
        cand_sc = sc

        if gmax > 1e-15:
            for maxlog in (0.05, 0.15, 0.4):
                eta = maxlog / gmax
                logit = -eta * rel
                logit = np.clip(logit, -10.0, 10.0)
                b = a * np.exp(logit)
                b = normalize_sequence(b)
                scb, _, _ = score_of_sequence(b, ffac)
                if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < cand_sc:
                    cand = b
                    cand_sc = scb

        D = min(32, n)
        if D > 0:
            contrib = a * grad
            if D < n:
                donors = np.argpartition(contrib, n - D)[-D:]
                receivers = np.argpartition(grad, D - 1)[:D]
            else:
                donors = np.arange(n)
                receivers = np.arange(n)

            for move_frac in (0.005, 0.02, 0.06):
                b = a.copy()
                don_sum = float(np.sum(b[donors]))
                mmove = min(float(move_frac), 0.5 * don_sum)
                if mmove <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                wd = b[donors]
                s = float(np.sum(wd))
                if s > 0.0:
                    b[donors] -= mmove * wd / s
                add = mmove / float(len(receivers))
                b[receivers] += add
                b = normalize_sequence(b)
                scb, _, _ = score_of_sequence(b, ffac)
                if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < cand_sc:
                    cand = b
                    cand_sc = scb

        if cand is not None:
            a = cand
            sc = cand_sc
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sc
                best = a.copy()
                stagnant = 0

        if cand is None and stagnant % 40 == 0:
            b = block_perturb(a, rng)
            scb, _, _ = score_of_sequence(b, ffac)
            if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < sc - 1e-12:
                a = b
                sc = scb
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best = a.copy()
                    stagnant = 0

    final_best = None
    final_sc = np.inf
    use_exact = 0 < n <= 12000

    final_arrays = [best]
    if parent is not None and parent.size == n and float(np.sum(parent)) > 0.0:
        final_arrays.append(parent)
    for arr in inits:
        if len(final_arrays) >= 8:
            break
        final_arrays.append(arr)

    for arr in final_arrays:
        if arr is None or arr.size != n:
            continue
        out = prepare_return(arr)
        sct = np.inf
        if use_exact:
            try:
                sct = float(evaluate_sequence(out.tolist()))
            except Exception:
                sct = np.inf
        if not np.isfinite(sct):
            sct, _, _ = score_of_sequence(out, ffac)
        if np.isfinite(sct) and sct < final_sc:
            final_sc = sct
            final_best = out

    if final_best is None:
        final_best = prepare_return(best)

    return [float(x) for x in final_best]
```
